#### Abstracts
##### **Invariant Learning Dynamics of Transformers in Inductive Reasoning Tasks**
2607.11875v1 by Tiberiu Musat, Tiago Pimentel, Nicholas Zucchet, Thomas Hofmann

We present a theoretical framework to explain the emergence of inductive reasoning abilities in Transformer language models. While previous works on Transformer learning dynamics have so far been mostly tied to specific tasks, we study a generalized class of inductive tasks that unifies several synthetic tasks known in the literature, including in-context n-grams and multi-hop reasoning. In this class, we theoretically prove that the training dynamics of attention models can be confined to a highly interpretable, low-dimensional invariant manifold. On this manifold, the learning dynamics are captured by a handful of interpretable coordinates rather than millions of parameters, making both theoretical and empirical analysis more tractable. Using this framework, we characterize how data statistics govern the competition between in-context and in-weights learning, we study how random initializations determine the `winning' circuit when multiple solutions are possible, and we demonstrate that the coordinate frame associated with the manifold can be used to automatically detect which circuits have been learned in trained models. By casting circuit formation as a low-dimensional dynamical phenomenon, we take a step toward a predictive theory of how Transformers learn.

摘要：我們提出了一個理論框架，以解釋Transformer語言模型中歸納推理能力的出現。<lb/>雖然先前關於Transformer學習動態的研究大多與特定任務相關，但我們研究了一類通用的歸納任務，該類任務統一了文獻中已知的幾個合成任務，包括上下文 n-gram 和多跳推理。<lb/>在這個類別中，我們理論上證明了注意力模型的訓練動態可以被限制在一個高度可解釋的低維不變流形上。<lb/>在這個流形上，學習動態是由少數可解釋的坐標捕捉，而不是數百萬個參數，這使得理論和實證分析變得更加可行。<lb/>利用這個框架，我們描述了數據統計如何主導上下文學習和權重學習之間的競爭，研究了隨機初始化如何決定在多個解可能的情況下“獲勝”的電路，並展示了與該流形相關的坐標框架如何用於自動檢測訓練模型中學習到的電路。<lb/>通過將電路形成視為一種低維動態現象，我們邁出了朝著預測Transformer學習方式的理論的一步。

##### **Evidence-Backed Video Question Answering**
2607.11862v1 by Shijie Wang, Honglu Zhou, Ziyang Wang, Ran Xu, Caiming Xiong, Silvio Savarese, Chen Sun, Juan Carlos Niebles

Despite their central role in fault detection, test oracles remain challenging to construct effectively. Rather than generating assertions, this study explores a different approach by training a model to directly predict whether a given test prefix passes or fails. It learns from labeled pairs of test prefixes and methods under test, employs losses that emphasize failing cases during training, and grounds its predictions in statement level behavioral evidence. Compared with the baseline method SEER, we substantially improve performance on failing cases for unseen projects and provide richer explanations. A preliminary evaluation on fault-detection benchmarks and automated test-generation artifacts shows that our approach is highly accurate within its training distribution and substantially improves failure detection on previously unseen projects where prior discriminative oracles collapse. Moreover, the highlighted statements are supported by behavioral explanation checks. These early results suggest that fail-aware discriminative oracle prediction can complement existing approaches such as fuzzing, search-based testing, and LLM-based test generation. These techniques produce test prefixes at scale but often lack fault oriented oracles. In future work, FOCAL could take generated test prefixes and attach fault-aware predicted oracles to them, turning high-volume input generation into executable tests that are more likely to expose semantic failures.

摘要：儘管在故障檢測中扮演著核心角色，測試預言仍然難以有效構建。最近的基於學習的方法通過自動生成測試斷言來應對這一挑戰，然而即使在語法上正確，它們通常在揭示錯誤方面效果不佳。與其生成斷言，本研究探索了一種不同的方法，通過訓練模型直接預測給定的測試前綴是通過還是失敗。我們提出了FOCAL，一種新興的基於代碼的LLM判別預言器。它從標記的測試前綴和被測試方法的對中學習，在訓練期間使用強調失敗案例的損失，並將其預測基於語句級的行為證據。與基線方法SEER相比，我們在未見項目的失敗案例上顯著提高了性能，並提供了更豐富的解釋。在故障檢測基準和自動測試生成工件上的初步評估顯示，我們的方法在其訓練分佈內具有高準確性，並在先前未見的項目上顯著改善了故障檢測，這些項目在先前的判別預言中失敗。此外，突出顯示的語句得到了行為解釋檢查的支持。這些早期結果表明，失敗感知的判別預言可以補充現有的方法，如模糊測試、基於搜索的測試和基於LLM的測試生成。這些技術以規模產生測試前綴，但往往缺乏面向故障的預言。在未來的工作中，FOCAL可以將生成的測試前綴與失敗感知的預測預言附加到一起，將高容量的輸入生成轉化為更有可能揭示語義失敗的可執行測試。

While existing reasoning step evaluators effectively detect logical fallacies and factual errors, our analysis reveals a critical blind spot: they fail to penalize valid but inefficient reasoning steps that inflate token usage without contributing to the solution. To systematically diagnose this limitation, we introduce RIV-GSM8K, a diagnostic benchmark injected with five distinct types of inefficiencies, including circular reasoning and excessive decomposition. Diagnostic experiments reveal that state-of-the-art evaluators struggle to distinguish these inefficiencies from necessary reasoning. ##### **Multiplication Beyond Groups: Stratified Fourier Mechanisms in Transformer Circuits**
2607.07066v1 by Zitong Andrew Chen, Junaid Hasan, Akhil Srinivasan, Hemkesh Bandi, Jarod Alper

Transformers have demonstrated a remarkable ability to learn algorithmic reasoning, yet mechanistic analyses have mostly focused on globally invertible operations such as cyclic addition and group composition. In this work, we investigate how small transformers learn modular integer multiplication over composite moduli, a fundamentally non-invertible operation due to the presence of zero-divisors. We propose the monoid extension: a localized generalization of Group Composition via Representation (GCR) that suggests the learned computation does not rely on a single global representation space. Instead, the model partitions the input space into local hierarchical algebraic regions, where group-like structure survives and Fourier mechanisms can be applied. In transformers trained on square-free modular multiplication, we find that embeddings organize around these regions, attention exhibits class-sensitive routing and low-rank write directions, and local character features explain a large fraction of the model's output logits. ##### **On the Principles of Deep Feedforward ReLU Networks**
2607.07035v1 by Changcun Huang

The architecture of deep feedforward neural networks is ubiquitous in deep learning, either as a whole system or as a subnetwork of other architectures, and thus its mechanism is a key ingredient of the black box of neural networks. On the basis of the simplest two-layer ReLU network, this paper systematically studies the mechanism of deep feedforward ReLU networks with multiple hidden layers and successfully explains the training solution obtained by the back-propagation algorithm. The concept of a path, especially in terms of the relationships between paths, plays a central role in uncovering the mystery of the black box. It is shown that a unit of a deep ReLU network can form a piecewise linear manifold to divide the input space, instead of a hyperplane of the two-layer case. How to efficiently use the hidden-layer units to produce both linear functions and partitions of the input space is also a central problem. The principles of a two-layer ReLU network can be generalized to the deeper case to a large extent, such as multiple strict partial orders and continuity restriction. The combination of the basic and simple principles proposed can yield complicated instantiations including the training solutions, and in this sense the black box of deep feedforward ReLU networks is revealed.

Deep learning image classifiers achieve strong predictive performance yet remain opaque in how decisions are formed. On large scale datasets this opacity is especially problematic, since inspecting heatmaps one sample at a time cannot scale to thousands of predictions. We propose Relevance Based Model Decision Explainability (ReMoDEx), a framework for systematic, dataset scale assessment of model decision behaviour in image classification. ReMoDEx defines a stepwise pipeline: model inference, target class selection, relevance map generation, heatmap standardisation, similarity based grouping of patterns, cluster level interpretation, and spatial relevance assessment. Local methods GradCAM++, Integrated Gradients, Occlusion Sensitivity, and Layerwise Relevance Propagation are each combined independently with a single global module that summarises an entire set of relevance maps into a few decision strategy clusters, replacing sample by sample inspection with an automatic, scalable summary. However, each explainer combined with the global module consistently produced two recurring strategies: central thoracic region decisions and border/corner sensitive decisions, indicating possible shortcut learning that conventional metrics could not reveal. Masked image validation confirmed that model confidence and predicted class changed when central or peripheral regions were occluded. ReMoDEx thus provides a scalable relevance based decision assessment framework and an essential complement to accuracy based evaluation.

While the attributions are not intended to fully recover the system's generative dynamics, they provide valuable, dependency-aware explanations supporting both human interpretation and downstream predictive and diagnostic tasks. Although demonstrated in industrial IoT security, our framework also applies to other high-dimensional cyber-physical and socio-technical systems requiring principled, structural explanations.

What does a discrete diffusion model learn: a denoiser, a score ratio, or a bridge plug-in predictor? For sequences with token-factorizing noise, the oracle projection yields three exact coordinates for the optimizer: denoiser, cavity (bridge plug-in), and score, with closed-form conversions among them. Every identity is verified numerically, without approximation, on an exactly solvable model.

Finally, we review emerging studies that integrate generative AI with FL-based IDS and discuss open challenges, including synthetic data quality, realistic traffic generation, dual-use adversarial risks, non-IID client distributions, communication-efficient model sharing, federated IDS benchmarking, and domain-specific LLMs for network security.

摘要：入侵檢測系統（IDS）對於監控網絡流量和識別現代網絡物理、物聯網（IoT）、企業及分散式網絡環境中的惡意活動至關重要。<lb/>然而，開發可靠的IDS模型仍然具有挑戰性，因為攻擊行為隨時間演變，現實數據集難以獲得，流量記錄可能不完整，攻擊類別常常不平衡，且隱私限制限制了集中數據收集。<lb/>最近在生成人工智慧（AI）和聯邦學習（FL）方面的進展為解決這些限制提供了新的機會。<lb/>生成模型可以支持異常檢測、合成流量生成、數據增強、數據插補、對抗性流量生成和IDS警報解釋。<lb/>FL使得分散式IDS訓練在不直接共享本地網絡流量的情況下進行，這使其適合於隱私敏感和地理分散的環境。<lb/>本調查提供了生成AI和FL技術在IDS中的結構化回顧。<lb/>我們首先總結了代表性的IDS研究方向，包括對抗性機器學習、基於異常的檢測、面向IoT的IDS、可解釋的IDS和基準數據集。<lb/>然後，我們根據模型系列和任務目標對生成AI在IDS中的應用進行分類，涵蓋基於自編碼器的模型、生成對抗網絡（GAN）、擴散模型和大型語言模型（LLM）。<lb/>最後，我們回顧了將生成AI與基於FL的IDS整合的最新研究，並討論了開放挑戰，包括合成數據質量、現實流量生成、雙重用途對抗風險、非獨立同分佈的客戶端分佈、通信高效的模型共享、聯邦IDS基準測試，以及針對網絡安全的領域特定LLM。

##### **Muon as a Residual Connection**
2607.01124v1 by Hao Huang

Muon has recently emerged as one of the most effective optimizers for training large neural networks, yet its empirical success has been explained from several different perspectives. In this paper, we propose a simple mechanistic interpretation: Muon can be understood as an implicit residual connection during training. Specifically, orthogonalizing the update can sacrifice some immediate gradient fidelity while improving representation preservation for downstream layers. We study this trade-off in controlled linear optimization settings, where Muon can learn representations that are slower to fit a local target but easier for downstream layers to exploit. Our results suggest a conceptual explanation for Muon and a design perspective for optimizers that balance local descent with downstream usability.

摘要：Muon 最近已成為訓練大型神經網絡最有效的優化器之一，但其實證成功已從幾個不同的角度進行了解釋。<lb/>在本文中，我們提出了一個簡單的機械性解釋：Muon 可以被理解為訓練過程中的一種隱式殘差連接。<lb/>具體而言，正交化更新可能會犧牲一些即時的梯度忠實度，同時改善下游層的表示保留。<lb/>我們在受控的線性優化環境中研究這種權衡，在這裡 Muon 可以學習到更慢適應局部目標但對下游層更易於利用的表示。<lb/>我們的結果為 Muon 提供了一個概念解釋，並為優化器的設計提供了一種平衡局部下降與下游可用性的視角。

##### **Cheap Code, Costly Judgment: A Case Study on Governable Agentic Software Engineering**
2607.01087v2 by James C. Davis, Paschal C. Amusuo, Tanmay Singla, Berk Çakar, Kirsten A. Davis

Generative AI is shifting software engineering from a practice organized around scarce implementation effort toward one organized around abundant, low-cost code production. This shift changes the central engineering problem: not whether AI can generate useful code, but how engineers organize architectures, tools, evidence, and feedback loops so that AI-mediated development remains inspectable, correctable, and maintainable.
  We study this problem through a first-person case study: a 12-week development effort in which a single expert software engineer used frontier AI coding agents to build a document accessibility remediation system. The empirical record comprises 88 contemporaneous field notes, 420 KLOC of production code, and 1.16 MLOC of tests, lints, supporting documentation, and agent tooling. From this record, we develop a candidate middle-range theory of governance conversion, expressed as a process model explaining how high-velocity agentic implementation becomes governable. The model explains how agentic implementation velocity surfaces recurring structural failure classes, and how engineering judgment sustains velocity by converting those failures into durable governance mechanisms. In contrast to existing governance models that derive controls from known obligations, governance conversion explains how controls are discovered from failures that become visible only during agentic work. We use our model to make testable predictions and to describe implications for software engineering research and practice.

摘要：生成式人工智慧正在將軟體工程的實踐從圍繞稀缺實作努力的方式轉變為圍繞豐富、低成本的程式碼產出方式。這一轉變改變了中心工程問題：不再是 AI 是否能生成有用的程式碼，而是工程師如何組織架構、工具、證據和反饋循環，以確保 AI 介導的開發保持可檢查、可修正和可維護。<lb/>  
我們通過一個第一人稱的案例研究來研究這個問題：一個為期 12 週的開發努力，其中一位專業軟體工程師使用前沿的 AI 編碼代理來建立一個文檔可及性修復系統。實證記錄包括 88 份當時的現場筆記、420 KLOC 的生產程式碼，以及 1.16 MLOC 的測試、靜態檢查、支持文檔和代理工具。從這個記錄中，我們發展出一個候選的中程治理轉換理論，表達為一個過程模型，解釋高速度代理實作如何變得可治理。該模型解釋了代理實作速度如何顯現出重複的結構失敗類別，以及工程判斷如何通過將這些失敗轉化為持久的治理機制來維持速度。與現有的治理模型不同，後者從已知的義務中派生控制，治理轉換解釋了如何從僅在代理工作中變得可見的失敗中發現控制。我們使用我們的模型來提出可測試的預測，並描述對軟體工程研究和實踐的影響。

##### **LongVQUBench: Benchmarking Long-Term Video Quality Understanding of Vision-Language Models**
2607.01086v1 by Arpita Nema, Hanwei Zhu, Xi Zhang, Weisi Lin

The evaluation of long-term video quality understanding remains an open challenge for large vision-language models (LVLMs). Existing video quality benchmarks predominantly focus on short clips and isolated distortions, overlooking the temporal continuity, cumulative degradation, and reasoning complexity inherent in long-duration content. To address these limitations, we present LongVQUBench, a comprehensive benchmark for long-term video quality understanding. LongVQUBench contains over 1200 diverse videos spanning movies, documentaries, surveillance footage, egocentric recordings, and animated content, accompanied by 1500 multiple-choice and open-ended questions for validation and testing. To assess perceptual reasoning across different temporal scopes, we introduce three progressively complex evaluation levels: (i) local event quality understanding (LQU) for analyzing localized distortions; (ii) cross-event quality reasoning (CQR) for integrating multiple degraded events; and (iii) global quality understanding (GQU) for holistic perceptual evaluation over extended durations. Furthermore, a needle distortion question-answering (NDQA) paradigm is embedded across all three levels, where spatial or temporal artifacts are sparsely inserted to probe fine-grained detection and reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments on 14 state-of-the-art LVLMs reveal significant performance degradation with increasing video length and reasoning depth, highlighting their limited capacity for long-range temporal integration and perceptual attribution. We envision LongVQUBench as a foundational step toward the systematic, hierarchical, and explainable evaluation of LVLMs' long-term video quality understanding.

摘要：長期視頻質量理解的評估仍然是大型視覺-語言模型（LVLMs）面臨的一個開放挑戰。現有的視頻質量基準主要集中在短片和孤立失真上，忽略了長時間內容固有的時間連續性、累積退化和推理複雜性。為了解決這些限制，我們提出了LongVQUBench，一個全面的長期視頻質量理解基準。LongVQUBench包含超過1200個多樣化的視頻，涵蓋電影、紀錄片、監控畫面、自我中心錄像和動畫內容，並附有1500個多選和開放式問題以進行驗證和測試。為了評估不同時間範疇的感知推理，我們引入了三個逐步複雜的評估級別：（i）局部事件質量理解（LQU）用於分析局部失真；（ii）跨事件質量推理（CQR）用於整合多個退化事件；以及（iii）全局質量理解（GQU）用於對延長時間的整體感知評估。此外，一個針對失真問題回答（NDQA）範式被嵌入到所有三個級別中，其中空間或時間工件被稀疏插入，以探測細粒度的檢測和推理能力。在14個最先進的LVLM上進行的廣泛實驗顯示，隨著視頻長度和推理深度的增加，性能顯著下降，突顯了它們在長距離時間整合和感知歸因方面的有限能力。我們將LongVQUBench視為邁向系統性、分層和可解釋的LVLM長期視頻質量理解評估的基礎性步驟。

##### **Staleness-Learning Rate Scaling Laws for Asynchronous RLHF**
2607.01083v2 by Jingwei Song, Haofeng Xu, Jie Xiao, Chengke Bao, Jingwei Shi, Pengbin Feng, Weixun Wang, Yuhang Han, Chuan Wu, Linfeng Zhang, Bill Shi

High-throughput RLHF systems often decouple rollout generation from policy optimization, leading to the use of stale rollouts during learner updates. In this work, we study the effect of such staleness in asynchronous GRPO. We make the behavior policy explicit in the GRPO surrogate objective and distinguish between the surrogate-gradient mapping used by the learner and the true total derivative of a distribution-dependent population objective. Under assumptions of local boundedness, distributional smoothness, and behavior-policy smoothness, we show that stale rollouts introduce a per-step surrogate-gradient bias of order O(S * eta), where S denotes the maximum rollout lag and eta denotes the learning rate. We further derive a conditional collapse-time scaling law: when within-cycle drift remains below a batch-level clipping radius, collapse is governed primarily by cumulative learner drift T * eta; when the stale-rollout constraint is active, stability instead depends explicitly on S * eta. This yields a two-constraint stability condition eta << min{R_batch / (S * G_upd), R_crit / (T * G_upd)}, explaining why the maximum stable learning rate may appear weakly dependent on staleness in the horizon-limited regime.

摘要：高通量的RLHF系統通常將回合生成與策略優化解耦，導致在學習者更新期間使用過時的回合。在這項工作中，我們研究了這種過時性在非同步GRPO中的影響。我們在GRPO的代理目標中明確了行為策略，並區分了學習者使用的代理梯度映射和依賴於分佈的總導數的群體目標。在局部有界性、分佈平滑性和行為策略平滑性的假設下，我們顯示過時的回合引入了每步代理梯度偏差的順序為O(S * eta)，其中S表示最大回合延遲，eta表示學習率。我們進一步推導出一個條件崩潰時間縮放定律：當循環內漂移保持在批次級別裁剪半徑以下時，崩潰主要由累積學習者漂移T * eta主導；當過時回合約束有效時，穩定性則明確依賴於S * eta。這導致了一個雙約束穩定性條件eta << min{R_batch / (S * G_upd), R_crit / (T * G_upd)}，解釋了為什麼最大穩定學習率在有限視野範圍內可能顯得對過時性弱依賴。

##### **CPG-PAD: Concept-Informed Prompts Guided Presentation Attack Detection**
2607.01303v1 by Haoyuan Zhang, Xiangyu Zhu, Li Gao, Ajian Liu, Siran Peng, Zhen Lei

Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) serves as a crucial safeguard for face recognition systems against presentation attacks such as printed photos, replayed videos, and 3D masks. Despite significant progress, existing PAD models still struggle to generalize across unseen domains due to variations in sensors, lighting, and attack materials. Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong generalization ability, yet their applications in PAD remain limited because learned prompts, typically optimized under class-label supervision, fail to explicitly align with fine-grained attack-relevant visual semantics. As a result, the learned representations often overfit domain-specific artifacts instead of capturing transferable attack cues. To address this, we propose Concept-Informed Prompts Guided Presentation Attack Detection (CPG-PAD), a framework that introduces model-level concept guidance into the prompt learning process. Specifically, we design a Visual Concept-driven Enhancement (VCE) module that employs eXplainable AI (XAI) techniques to automatically discover PAD-relevant visual concepts and generate concept-associated heatmaps providing localized fine-grained guidance. Guided by these heatmaps, a Prompt-based Concept Injection (PCI) mechanism integrates these concepts into the prompt space through a Visual-Prompt Decoder (VPD) and a concept-mapping loss, enabling prompts to align with the model's internal concept space. This design enables CPG-PAD to capture generalizable and domain-invariant attack cues while effectively suppressing dataset-specific biases. Extensive experiments across nine benchmark datasets demonstrate that CPG-PAD consistently achieves state-of-the-art cross-domain performance under multi-source, limited-source, and single-source settings.

摘要：Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) 作為面部識別系統對抗展示攻擊（如印刷照片、重播視頻和3D面具）的重要保障。<lb/>儘管取得了顯著進展，但現有的 PAD 模型仍然難以在未見過的領域中進行泛化，這是由於傳感器、照明和攻擊材料的變化。<lb/>最近的視覺-語言模型（VLMs）顯示出強大的泛化能力，然而它們在 PAD 中的應用仍然有限，因為學習到的提示通常是在類別標籤監督下優化的，未能明確對齊與細粒度攻擊相關的視覺語義。<lb/>因此，學習到的表示往往過度擬合於特定領域的工件，而不是捕捉可轉移的攻擊線索。<lb/>為了解決這個問題，我們提出了概念引導的提示引導展示攻擊檢測（CPG-PAD），這是一個將模型級概念指導引入提示學習過程的框架。<lb/>具體而言，我們設計了一個視覺概念驅動的增強（VCE）模塊，該模塊利用可解釋的人工智慧（XAI）技術自動發現與 PAD 相關的視覺概念並生成與概念相關的熱圖，提供局部的細粒度指導。<lb/>在這些熱圖的指導下，基於提示的概念注入（PCI）機制通過視覺提示解碼器（VPD）和概念映射損失將這些概念整合到提示空間中，使提示與模型的內部概念空間對齊。<lb/>這一設計使得 CPG-PAD 能夠捕捉可泛化和領域不變的攻擊線索，同時有效抑制數據集特定的偏差。<lb/>在九個基準數據集上進行的廣泛實驗表明，CPG-PAD 在多源、有限源和單源設置下始終實現了最先進的跨域性能。

##### **Behavior-Adaptive Conversational Agents: Toward a Fluid Personality Framework**
2607.01034v1 by Hasibur Rahman, Smit Desai

Large language model (LLM)-based conversational agents (CAs) are now ubiquitous, creating new opportunities for AI-mediated behavior change. Their capacity to project nuanced personalities and adopt diverse metaphorical roles raises a design question: how should an agent's persona and personality be calibrated to the moment? Recent evidence suggests that (i) moderate personality expression outperforms low or high extremes on trust, enjoyment, and intention to adopt in goal-oriented tasks, and (ii) context-appropriate metaphors outperform static one-note assistants on user experience and uptake. Yet most CAs still fix both persona and style, risking misalignment when dynamics, urgency, and formality vary, for example in medical information seeking, fitness coaching, and reflective learning. We propose a Fluid Personality Framework that jointly adapts (1) the agent's metaphorical persona, such as coach, tutor, librarian, or tool, and (2) its personality expression intensity, low, medium, or high, as a function of task context, user goals and traits, and situational urgency. We sketch the framework and its core design dimensions.

摘要：大型語言模型（LLM）基礎的對話代理（CAs）現在無處不在，為 AI 介導的行為改變創造了新的機會。<lb/>它們展現出細緻的個性並採用多樣的隱喻角色，提出了一個設計問題：代理的角色和個性應如何根據當下進行調整？<lb/>最近的證據表明，(i) 適度的個性表達在信任、享受和採用意圖方面優於低或高的極端表達，並且 (ii) 上下文適當的隱喻在用戶體驗和接受度方面優於靜態的單一助手。<lb/>然而，大多數 CAs 仍然固定了角色和風格，當動態、緊迫性和正式性變化時，可能會導致不一致，例如在尋求醫療資訊、健身指導和反思學習中。<lb/>我們提出了一個流動個性框架，該框架共同調整 (1) 代理的隱喻角色，如教練、導師、圖書館員或工具，以及 (2) 其個性表達的強度，低、中或高，作為任務上下文、用戶目標和特徵以及情境緊迫性的函數。<lb/>我們勾勒出該框架及其核心設計維度。

#### Abstracts
##### **A Minimalist Retargeting-Guided Reinforcement Learning Recipe for Dexterous Manipulation**
2607.11874v1 by Yunhai Feng, Natalie Leung, Jiaxuan Wang, Lujie Yang, Haozhi Qi, Preston Culbertson

Recent work in humanoid whole-body control has found success with a simple recipe: retarget human motion to robot kinematic references, then train policies via reinforcement learning (RL) to track them. But how does this recipe transfer to dexterous manipulation? The answer is not obvious, as manipulation involves complex, contact-rich dynamics and requires delicate regulation of contact modes and forces. We present REGRIND, a minimalist retargeting-guided RL pipeline that learns dexterous manipulation policies from a single human demonstration. REGRIND retargets human hand-object motion to a robot reference that preserves hand-object spatial and contact relationships, trains a residual RL policy in simulation to track object-centric keypoints along that reference, and transfers the resulting policy zero-shot to hardware with careful system identification. The resulting policies produce fluid, human-like behavior on two different multi-fingered hands across contact-rich tool-use tasks, including operating a pair of scissors and turning a screwdriver. Through systematic hardware experiments, we identify and analyze the key factors that govern sim-to-real transfer in dexterous manipulation, offering practical guidance for retargeting-based learning in contact-rich settings. Videos and code are available at https://yunhaifeng.com/REGRIND.

摘要：最近在類人全身控制方面的研究取得了成功，使用了一個簡單的配方：將人類運動重新定向到機器人的運動學參考，然後通過強化學習（RL）訓練策略以跟踪它們。<lb/>但這個配方如何轉移到靈巧操作上呢？答案並不明顯，因為操作涉及複雜且接觸豐富的動力學，並需要對接觸模式和力量進行精細調節。<lb/>我們提出了REGRIND，一個極簡的重新定向引導的RL管道，從單一的人類示範中學習靈巧操作策略。<lb/>REGRIND將人類手部與物體的運動重新定向到一個保留手部與物體空間和接觸關係的機器人參考，並在模擬中訓練一個殘差RL策略，以沿著該參考跟踪以物體為中心的關鍵點，然後通過仔細的系統識別將結果策略零樣本轉移到硬體上。<lb/>所得到的策略在兩種不同的多指手上產生流暢的人類般行為，涵蓋了接觸豐富的工具使用任務，包括操作一把剪刀和轉動一個螺絲刀。<lb/>通過系統的硬體實驗，我們識別並分析了影響靈巧操作中模擬到現實轉移的關鍵因素，為接觸豐富環境中的重新定向學習提供了實用指導。<lb/>視頻和代碼可在 https://yunhaifeng.com/REGRIND 獲得。

##### **LoRA-Based Cascaded Multimodal Fusion for Action Recognition in Medical Training Environments**
2607.11839v1 by Divya Mereddy, Jeevan Beedareddy

This paper presents a cascaded Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA)-based multimodal fusion framework for action and activity recognition in healthcare-oriented training environments. The proposed architecture combines parameter-efficient modality-specific adaptation with sequential fusion, enabling modalities to be integrated in stages without retraining previously learned components. Rather than assuming a fixed fusion structure, the framework first integrates more closely related modalities and then incorporates additional heterogeneous modalities, supporting scalable adaptation across datasets with different modality sets.We evaluate the framework on two healthcare-oriented training environment datasets: NurViD and the Nurse Training dataset. Across these datasets, preliminary results suggest that the proposed cascaded fusion strategy improves over individual modality models and provides competitive performance relative to previously reported dataset-specific baselines. Overall, these findings indicate that cascaded LoRA-based fusion is a promising parameter-efficient approach for integrating heterogeneous modalities in medical training action and activity recognition tasks. github: https://github.com/anonymous0-ai/LoRA-Based-Cascaded-Multimodal-Fusion-.git.

摘要：這篇論文提出了一個基於級聯低秩適應（LoRA）的多模態融合框架，用於醫療導向訓練環境中的行為和活動識別。<lb/>所提出的架構結合了參數高效的模態特定適應與序列融合，使得模態可以分階段整合，而無需重新訓練先前學習的組件。<lb/>該框架並不假設固定的融合結構，而是首先整合更密切相關的模態，然後再納入其他異質模態，支持在具有不同模態集的數據集上進行可擴展的適應。<lb/>我們在兩個醫療導向訓練環境數據集上評估了該框架：NurViD和護理訓練數據集。<lb/>在這些數據集中，初步結果表明，所提出的級聯融合策略相較於單一模態模型有所改善，並且相對於先前報告的數據集特定基準提供了具有競爭力的性能。<lb/>總體而言，這些發現表明，基於級聯LoRA的融合是一種有前景的參數高效方法，適用於在醫療訓練行為和活動識別任務中整合異質模態。<lb/>github: https://github.com/anonymous0-ai/LoRA-Based-Cascaded-Multimodal-Fusion-.git

##### **RAGU: A Multi-Step GraphRAG Engine with a Compact Domain-Adapted LLM**
2607.11683v1 by Mikhail Komarov, Ivan Bondarenko, Stanislav Shtuka, Oleg Sedukhin, Roman Shuvalov, Yana Dementyeva, Matvey Solovyov, Nikolay O. Nikitin

Graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) enhances large language models with structured knowledge, yet existing systems construct knowledge graphs in a single extraction pass, producing noisy entities and brittle retrieval. RAGU, an open-source modular GraphRAG engine, addresses this by separating extraction from consolidation: entities and relations pass through two-stage typed extraction, DBSCAN-backed deduplication, LLM summarization, and Leiden community detection. A key insight motivates a compact extractor: the skills an in-pipeline LLM needs - comprehension, extraction, reasoning over context - are language skills that grow only weakly with model size, unlike factual world knowledge. Accordingly, we train Meno-Lite-0.1, a 7B model optimized for language skills, which outperforms Qwen2.5-32B on knowledge-graph construction (+12.5% relative harmonic mean) and matches it on English GraphRAG tasks. On GraphRAG-Bench (Medical), RAGU retrieves the most complete context at every factoid level (evidence recall up to 0.84 vs. $\leq$0.76) and overtakes HippoRAG2 on synthesis tasks; on multi-hop factoid QA, the apparent HippoRAG2 advantage is shown to be largely an answer-format artifact. RAGU is installable via $\texttt{pip install graph_ragu}$, runs on a single GPU, and is released under MIT. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/RaguTeam/RAGU, and the Meno-Lite-0.1 model can be obtained from https://huggingface.co/bond005/meno-lite-0.1.

To ensure clinical relevance, we adopt the nnU-Net architecture and train it on a publicly available dataset collected in our study with pixel-level annotations for three liver cancer types: hepatocellular cacrcinoma (HCC; 55 images from 30 patients), cholangiocellular carcinoma (CCA; 55 images from 29 patients), and colorectal metastatic adenocarcinoma (CMA; 60 images from 30 patients). Annotations were independently provided by four pathologist. We hypothesize that the combination of stain normalization and semantic segmentation mitigates domain shift and reduces sensitivity to annotation noise. Five-fold cross-validation yielded balanced accuracy of 0.975 (HCC), 0.950 (CCA), and 1.000 (CMA), comparable to results obtained with immunohosthochemical staining and superior to several deep learning models trained on patch-level annotations. The proposed framework has the potential to support pathologists in prioritizing immunohistochemical marker selection, thereby reducing diagnostic costs and turnaround time. Integration with immunohistochemical findings improve overall diagnostic reliability.

摘要：由於蘇木精-伊紅（H&E）染色是常規診斷工作流程的主要切入點，因此基於全幻燈片H&E圖像的計算機輔助診斷具有特別的臨床相關性。<lb/>然而，樣本準備、染色方案和掃描條件的顯著變異，加上專家像素級標註的固有不確定性，使得H&E染色圖像的自動分析變得具有挑戰性。<lb/>在本研究中，我們提出了一種基於語義分割的框架，用於圖像級診斷，這一框架基於臨床驅動的假設，即每幅組織病理圖像對應於一種癌症類型。<lb/>圖像級預測是通過將分割輸出中佔主導地位的像素級標籤的類別分配給圖像來獲得的。<lb/>為了確保臨床相關性，我們採用了nnU-Net架構，並在我們研究中收集的公開數據集上進行訓練，該數據集包含三種肝癌類型的像素級標註：肝細胞癌（HCC；來自30名患者的55幅圖像）、膽管細胞癌（CCA；來自29名患者的55幅圖像）和結直腸轉移性腺癌（CMA；來自30名患者的60幅圖像）。<lb/>標註由四位病理學家獨立提供。我們假設染色標準化和語義分割的結合可以減輕領域轉移並降低對標註噪聲的敏感性。<lb/>五折交叉驗證的結果顯示，HCC的平衡準確率為0.975，CCA為0.950，CMA為1.000，這些結果與免疫組織化學染色的結果相當，並且優於幾個基於補丁級標註訓練的深度學習模型。<lb/>所提出的框架有潛力支持病理學家優先選擇免疫組織化學標記，從而降低診斷成本和周轉時間。<lb/>與免疫組織化學發現的整合提高了整體診斷的可靠性。

##### **SNR-Adaptive Unified Diffusion for Multi-Task Medical Image Segmentation**
2607.03103v1 by Jiahao Liu, Hang Wei, Shuai Wu

Clinical cardiac imaging pipelines currently deploy separate models for each dataset and modality, incurring redundant training costs and precluding knowledge sharing across anatomically related tasks. Consolidating semi-supervised learning, unsupervised domain adaptation, and domain generalisation into one model is therefore a practical necessity, yet naive joint training exposes a fundamental barrier: conflicting label semantics between datasets collapse LA Dice from 90.31\% to 83.38\%, while gradient imbalance across tasks of unequal complexity suppresses the weaker tasks throughout training. We present UniT-Diff, a unified diffusion segmentation framework that resolves these conflicts through three targeted mechanisms. An 11-channel task-specific output space physically partitions label categories, eliminating cross-task gradient sign reversal by construction. SNR-Adaptive Task Conditioning (SATC) scales the task token by the log signal-to-noise ratio of the current diffusion timestep, suppressing domain-specific bias during coarse denoising and restoring full task guidance as the signal clears. Task-Type-Aware Conditional Dropout (TTACD) permanently removes the task token for domain-generalisation inputs, routing them through a shared neutral pathway that draws on cross-dataset cardiac anatomy rather than source-vendor statistics. Under a single parameter set, UniT-Diff surpasses independently trained task-specific baselines on all three benchmarks simultaneously: +0.87\% on LA, +1.77\% on MMWHS, and +0.88\% on MNMS.

摘要：臨床心臟影像處理流程目前為每個數據集和模態部署了單獨的模型，這導致了冗餘的訓練成本，並阻礙了在解剖相關任務之間的知識共享。因此，將半監督學習、無監督領域適應和領域泛化整合為一個模型是實際上的必要，但天真的聯合訓練暴露出一個根本性障礙：數據集之間衝突的標籤語義使得左心房Dice從90.31\%降至83.38\%，而不同複雜度任務之間的梯度不平衡在整個訓練過程中抑制了較弱的任務。我們提出了UniT-Diff，一個統一的擴散分割框架，通過三個針對性的機制解決這些衝突。11通道的任務特定輸出空間在物理上劃分了標籤類別，通過結構消除了跨任務梯度符號反轉。SNR自適應任務條件（SATC）根據當前擴散時間步的對數信噪比縮放任務標記，抑制了粗糙去噪過程中的領域特定偏差，並在信號清晰時恢復完整的任務指導。任務類型感知條件隨機失活（TTACD）對於領域泛化輸入永久移除任務標記，將其通過一個共享的中立通道路由，該通道依賴於跨數據集的心臟解剖學，而非源供應商統計。在單一參數集下，UniT-Diff在所有三個基準上同時超越了獨立訓練的任務特定基準：左心房+0.87\%，MMWHS+1.77\%，MNMS+0.88\%。

##### **PixCon: Clean-Positive Contrastive Learning for Foundation-Model Semi-Supervised Segmentation**
2607.03068v1 by Ebenezer Tarubinga

Semi-supervised semantic segmentation (SSSS) has long turned on one question, which pseudo-labels to trust, and answered it with ever more careful confidence filtering. Foundation backbones change the regime: with a DINOv2 teacher a strict threshold already retains a measured 98%-clean pseudo-label set, so the accuracy that remains lives not in the filter but in how the embedding space is structured by class. We propose PixCon, a clean-positive pixel-contrastive framework. PixCon maintains a per-class memory bank that admits only labeled pixels the student already classifies correctly, guaranteeing a contamination-free positive set ($ρ_F=0$) by construction, unlike prior contrastive SSSS banks (ReCo, U$^2$PL) built from confidence-filtered pseudo-labels. It is a single branch over a consistency backbone, adds no inference-time parameters, and needs no bank-specific threshold. A first-order analysis of the supervised-InfoNCE gradient explains why contamination hurts: its false-positive term scales as $ρ_F/(1-ρ_F)$, which we measure (0.018 on Pascal, 0.106 on ADE20K) rather than assume. Across Pascal VOC, Cityscapes, and ADE20K, PixCon matches or improves a strong DINOv2-based UniMatch V2 baseline in a compute-matched one-switch protocol: it improves every Pascal-1/8 seed (a per-seed gain of about +0.2 mIoU) and its three-seed mean reaches 87.90, the published UniMatch V2-B figure. Because contamination is already rare under foundation-model teachers, our analysis indicates the $ρ_F=0$ guarantee acts chiefly as robustness as teachers weaken, while the accuracy gain comes from cleaner positive supervision, making clean-positive contrast a robust, low-cost default for foundation-model SSSS.

摘要：半監督語義分割（SSSS）長期以來一直圍繞著一個問題，即信任哪些偽標籤，並通過越來越謹慎的信心過濾來回答這個問題。基礎骨幹改變了這一規則：使用 DINOv2 教師時，嚴格的閾值已經保留了一組經過測量的 98% 乾淨偽標籤集，因此剩餘的準確性不在於過濾器，而在於類別如何結構化嵌入空間。我們提出了 PixCon，一個乾淨的正像素對比框架。PixCon 維持一個每類的記憶庫，只接受學生已正確分類的標記像素，通過構造保證了一個無污染的正集（$ρ_F=0$），這與先前基於信心過濾偽標籤構建的對比 SSSS 銀行（ReCo, U$^2$PL）不同。它是一個單支路，建立在一致性骨幹之上，不增加任何推理時間的參數，也不需要特定於銀行的閾值。對監督-InfoNCE 梯度的一階分析解釋了為什麼污染會造成傷害：其假陽性項的比例為 $ρ_F/(1-ρ_F)$，我們測量到的數值（在 Pascal 上為 0.018，在 ADE20K 上為 0.106），而不是假設。在 Pascal VOC、Cityscapes 和 ADE20K 中，PixCon 在計算匹配的一鍵協議中與強大的基於 DINOv2 的 UniMatch V2 基線相匹配或有所改善：它改善了每個 Pascal-1/8 的種子（每個種子的增益約為 +0.2 mIoU），其三個種子的平均值達到 87.90，這是已發表的 UniMatch V2-B 數字。由於在基礎模型教師下污染已經很少，我們的分析表明 $ρ_F=0$ 的保證主要作為穩健性，隨著教師的減弱，而準確性的增益來自更乾淨的正向監督，使得乾淨的正對比成為基礎模型 SSSS 的一個穩健、低成本的默認選擇。

##### **CONFLUX: A Latent Diffusion Model for 3D Chest-CT Synthesis with RL Post-Training**
2607.02998v2 by Max Van Puyvelde, Halil Ibrahim Gulluk, Wim Van Criekinge, Olivier Gevaert

Controllable generative models of 3D medical images can synthesize volumes with specified clinical attributes, but this demands samples that are simultaneously high-fidelity, natively 3D, and faithful to the requested conditioning. We present CONFLUX, a latent diffusion model for chest computed tomography (CT): a 3D variational autoencoder compresses each volume, and a rectified-flow transformer generates in the latent space. Generation is conditioned on structured radiological metadata (18 abnormality findings, sex, age, and reconstruction kernel) through adaptive layer normalization. The model leads strong volumetric baselines on tri-planar Frechet distance (FID 32.3 vs. 74.6 for MAISI) while exposing direct control over clinical attributes. To strengthen that control we add an online reinforcement-learning post-training stage (group-relative policy optimization) that rewards how reliably a classifier recovers the requested findings from each generated volume. We release the model and a ~200k synthetic chest-CT dataset with conditioning metadata spanning a wide variety of clinical findings.

摘要：可控的3D醫療影像生成模型可以合成具有指定臨床特徵的體積，但這需要同時具備高保真度、原生3D且忠實於請求條件的樣本。我們提出了CONFLUX，一種用於胸部電腦斷層掃描（CT）的潛在擴散模型：一個3D變分自編碼器壓縮每個體積，而一個修正流變換器在潛在空間中生成。生成是基於結構化的放射學元數據（18種異常發現、性別、年齡和重建內核）通過自適應層正規化進行條件化。該模型在三平面Frechet距離上達到了強大的體積基準（FID 32.3，相對於MAISI的74.6），同時對臨床特徵提供了直接控制。為了加強這種控制，我們增加了一個在線強化學習後訓練階段（群體相對政策優化），該階段獎勵分類器從每個生成的體積中可靠地恢復請求的發現。根據一個獨立的分類器評估，後訓練消除了相對於真實掃描可靠性的47%的不足。我們發布了該模型和一個約20萬的合成胸部CT數據集，該數據集包含涵蓋各種臨床發現的條件元數據。

##### **Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Seeking Diagnostic Reasoning with Large Language Models**
2607.02983v1 by Shengyi Hua, Kangzhe Hu, Conghui He, Xiaofan Zhang, Shaoting Zhang

Recent reasoning-centric Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant strides, yet they predominantly operate on a passive-inference pattern that assumes complete information. In contrast, real-world clinical intelligence is inherently an iterative investigative process requiring strategic evidence acquisition. To bridge this gap, we formalize medical diagnosis as an Iterative Evidence-Seeking Task. We leverage Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) to elicit intrinsic reasoning within a closed-loop environment, guided by a novel suite of rewards that enforce diagnostic precision and examination consistency. To facilitate this, we introduce the Retrieval-Augmented Generation-based Examination Simulator (RAGES), a high-fidelity clinical oracle that provides realistic, knowledge-grounded follow-up evidence. Empirical results across diverse datasets demonstrate that our framework enables LLMs to transition from passive responders to autonomous assistants. Notably, our model demonstrates comparable performance to larger and reasoning-enhanced baselines, while RAGES proves superior to vanilla LLMs in generating biologically plausible clinical feedback.

摘要：最近以推理為中心的大型語言模型（LLMs）取得了重大進展，但它們主要以被動推理模式運作，假設擁有完整的信息。相對而言，現實世界中的臨床智能本質上是一個需要戰略性證據獲取的迭代調查過程。為了彌補這一差距，我們將醫療診斷形式化為一個迭代證據尋求任務。我們利用可驗證獎勵的強化學習（RLVR）來引發封閉環境中的內在推理，並由一套新穎的獎勵系統指導，以強化診斷精確性和檢查一致性。為了促進這一過程，我們引入了基於檢索增強生成的考試模擬器（RAGES），這是一個高保真的臨床神諭，提供現實的、知識基礎的後續證據。來自多樣數據集的實證結果顯示，我們的框架使LLMs能夠從被動回應者轉變為自主助手。值得注意的是，我們的模型在性能上與更大且增強推理的基準相當，而RAGES在生成生物學上合理的臨床反饋方面優於普通的LLMs。

#### Abstracts
##### **Metacognition in LLMs: Foundations, Progress, and Opportunities**
2607.11881v1 by Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu, Areeb Gani, Jacqueline Lu, Jordan Thomas, Mark Steyvers, Arman Cohan

Metacognition is a foundational component of intelligence critical to effective learning, problem solving, decision-making, communication, and more. In recent years, it has become increasingly recognized as a cornerstone of capable, transparent AI systems. Yet while LLMs have made significant progress across diverse real-world tasks, it is not yet clear when, how, or to what extent they can exhibit or be endowed with effective metacognitive abilities, nor how such abilities can be adapted to advance the fundamental capabilities, reliability, and intelligence of AI systems. This paper bridges this gap by presenting the first comprehensive overview of the current state of knowledge on metacognition for LLMs. We analyze and taxonomize the landscape of this emerging field and summarize recent technical advancements, including methods and benchmarks to measure and evaluate LLMs' metacognitive abilities, techniques to elicit, improve, and apply metacognition in LLMs, and findings and implications of ongoing research. We also discuss applications, open questions and challenges, and promising directions for future work. Our aim is to provide a detailed and up-to-date review of this topic and stimulate meaningful research and discussion. An organized list of papers can be found at https://github.com/yale-nlp/LLM-Metacognition.

摘要：元認知是智力的基礎組成部分，對於有效的學習、問題解決、決策、溝通等至關重要。近年來，它越來越被認為是有能力且透明的人工智慧系統的基石。然而，儘管大型語言模型在多樣的現實世界任務中取得了顯著進展，但目前尚不清楚它們何時、如何或在多大程度上能夠展現或被賦予有效的元認知能力，以及這些能力如何能夠適應以提升人工智慧系統的基本能力、可靠性和智力。本文通過呈現有關大型語言模型元認知的當前知識狀態的首個綜合概述來填補這一空白。我們分析並對這一新興領域的格局進行分類，並總結近期的技術進展，包括測量和評估大型語言模型元認知能力的方法和基準、在大型語言模型中引發、改善和應用元認知的技術，以及正在進行的研究的發現和啟示。我們還討論了應用、未解決的問題和挑戰，以及未來工作的有希望方向。我們的目標是提供這一主題的詳細和最新的回顧，並激發有意義的研究和討論。組織好的論文列表可以在 https://github.com/yale-nlp/LLM-Metacognition 找到。

##### **How Temperature Shapes Ideological Discourse in Retrieval-Augmented Generation?**
2607.11783v1 by Elmira Salari, Hazem Amamou, José Victor de Souza, Shruti Kshirsagar, Maria Nunes Delfino, Anderson Avila

Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has been increasingly adopted to reduce hallucinations and strengthen the factual grounding of large language models (LLMs). While robustness to errors in the retrieval process has been explored, the impact of ideological bias on LLM outputs has been overlooked. For instance, if the retrieved material contains ideological positions, the RAG may transmit, amplify, or suppress such ideological discourses in its outputs. In this study, we address this issue by examining the influence of the RAG framework, comprising ideological discourses, in LLM-generated answers. To this end, we applied Lexical Multidimensional Analysis (LMDA) on a corpus of 1,117 COVID-19 treatment articles, identifying three ideological discourses. This corpus is then used as the external knowledge source for the RAG. We assessed several LLMs by having the models answer ideological questions at different sampling temperatures. The generated texts were assessed semantically and lexically based on their similarities with ideological reference texts. Our findings show that the RAG framework is prone to transferring ideological discourses into LLM responses, with sampling temperature having a measurable impact on the strength of this transfer. Discoursive alignment between generated answers and the reference text is highest at moderate temperatures, where models balance stochasticity with retrieval grounding, and drops at low temperatures, indicating that overly deterministic sampling suppresses discourse transfer.

摘要：檢索增強生成（RAG）越來越被採用，以減少幻覺並加強大型語言模型（LLMs）的事實基礎。雖然對檢索過程中錯誤的穩健性已經進行了探索，但意識形態偏見對LLM輸出的影響卻被忽視了。例如，如果檢索到的材料包含意識形態立場，RAG可能會在其輸出中傳遞、放大或抑制這些意識形態話語。在本研究中，我們通過檢查RAG框架中意識形態話語對LLM生成答案的影響來解決這一問題。為此，我們對1,117篇COVID-19治療文章的語料庫應用了詞彙多維分析（LMDA），識別出三種意識形態話語。然後將此語料庫用作RAG的外部知識來源。我們通過讓模型在不同的取樣溫度下回答意識形態問題來評估幾個LLM。生成的文本根據其與意識形態參考文本的相似性在語義和詞彙上進行了評估。我們的研究結果顯示，RAG框架容易將意識形態話語轉移到LLM的回答中，而取樣溫度對這種轉移的強度有可測量的影響。在中等溫度下，生成的答案與參考文本之間的話語一致性最高，模型在隨機性與檢索基礎之間取得平衡，而在低溫度下則下降，這表明過於確定性的取樣會抑制話語轉移。

##### **Active Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning**
2607.11720v1 by Alper Kamil Bozkurt, Shangtong Zhang, Yuichi Motai

Background: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables effective policies to be trained from large, previously collected datasets and subsequently improved through limited online interaction. This offline-to-online RL (O2O-RL) paradigm is particularly promising in nonstationary domains where interaction is costly or potentially hazardous. Standard O2O-RL pipelines train multiple candidate policies offline, evaluate them using off-policy or online evaluation, and then deploy and fine-tune the policy with the highest estimated value. However, as in offline pretraining, fine-tuning performance is highly sensitive to the choice of algorithm and hyperparameters, making it risky to commit to a single policy. Objectives: We study active policy selection for fine-tuning under a limited interaction budget in O2O-RL settings. To our knowledge, this is the first work to address this problem. Methods: We formulate the problem by identifying a fundamental trade-off between allocating online interactions to policy evaluation, which helps identify high-performing policies, and allocating them to fine-tuning, which improves policy performance. We then propose an approach that balances this trade-off by actively selecting policies for fine-tuning based on upper-confidence bounds on their future performance. These bounds are derived from locally linear performance forecasts fitted to observations obtained through online evaluation. Results: Across a diverse range of experiments, the proposed approach consistently outperforms existing O2O-RL baselines. Conclusions: Actively selecting and fine-tuning policies uses limited online interaction budgets more effectively than either committing to a single policy or dividing the budget equally among all policies. Our framework also advances offline RL toward practical deployment in real-world systems where online interaction is costly or risky.

摘要：背景：離線強化學習（RL）使得能夠從大量先前收集的數據集中訓練有效的策略，並通過有限的在線互動進行後續改進。這種離線到在線的強化學習（O2O-RL）範式在互動成本高或潛在危險的非穩定領域中特別有前景。標準的O2O-RL流程離線訓練多個候選策略，通過離線或在線評估進行評估，然後部署並微調估計價值最高的策略。然而，與離線預訓練一樣，微調性能對算法和超參數的選擇非常敏感，這使得承諾於單一策略變得風險很大。目標：我們研究在O2O-RL環境下，有限互動預算下的主動策略選擇以進行微調。據我們所知，這是第一個解決此問題的工作。方法：我們通過確定在線互動分配給策略評估（有助於識別高性能策略）和分配給微調（改善策略性能）之間的基本權衡來制定問題。我們然後提出一種方法，通過根據對其未來性能的上置信界主動選擇策略來平衡這一權衡。這些界限是從適合通過在線評估獲得的觀察結果的局部線性性能預測中推導出來的。結果：在多種實驗中，所提出的方法始終超越現有的O2O-RL基準。結論：主動選擇和微調策略比承諾於單一策略或在所有策略之間平均分配預算更有效地利用有限的在線互動預算。我們的框架還推進了離線RL向實際部署的發展，特別是在在線互動成本高或風險大的現實系統中。

##### **Agent Hacks Agent: Autoresearch for Production-Agent Red-Teaming**
2607.11698v1 by Xutao Mao, Xiang Zheng, Cong Wang

Production LLM agents such as Claude Code and Codex operate over untrusted content, files, commands, and workspace state, making safety failures directly actionable. Red-teaming must therefore keep pace with evolving models and tools. Existing approaches mainly optimize attack success and preserve artifacts such as benchmarks, payloads, or attack programs, which record where attacks succeed but not the enabling conditions behind unsafe agent behavior. We study automated red-teaming for production LLM agents using one agentic research environment to discover reusable vulnerability knowledge about another. We present AHA, a falsifiable discovery loop that proposes a vulnerability hypothesis, constructs a falsifier, instantiates a valid attack, executes it in a sandboxed harness, reflects on the trajectory, and promotes confirmed findings into a Vulnerability Concept Graph (VCG). Each concept links an attacker-facing surface to an unsafe trajectory through a claim, enabling condition, falsifier, transfer prediction, and supporting evidence. Across Claude Code and Codex on three scenarios covering direct and indirect attacks, the discovered concepts reveal a reusable vulnerability core across models and agents. A frozen VCG requires no further search and outperforms the strongest frozen discovery baseline by 14.2 percentage points under the same single-shot protocol, while transferring across scenarios and attack channels. The resulting VCG provides an auditable artifact for production safety teams to inspect vulnerabilities, validate patches, and accumulate reusable safety knowledge. Our code is available at https://github.com/henrymao2004/Auto-research-red-teaming-in-sleep.

Causal discovery, the process of recovering underlying causal structures from observational data, is a fundamental pursuit across scientific disciplines. Over the past decades, numerous algorithms have been developed to tackle this challenge through workflows tailored to the specific causal mechanisms underlying each type of dataset, demonstrating effectiveness across a wide range of applications. However, as the volume and heterogeneity of real-world data continue to grow, this dataset-specific approach inevitably leads to a fragmented, test-driven paradigm that struggles to scale to the demands of modern scientific discovery. To address this, we formulate the Causal Discovery Foundation Model (CDFM) as a unified, general-purpose framework for zero-shot structural inference. The variational decomposition provides a conceptual design principle for the architecture design of CDFM, while comprehensive causal knowledge guides the large-scale synthesis of our pretraining data. Our experiments show that LLMs exhibit substantial performance degradation on CRiT-QA compared to standard datasets, exposing their vulnerability to counterfactual conditions and distractor traps. #### Abstracts
##### **Metacognition in LLMs: Foundations, Progress, and Opportunities**
2607.11881v1 by Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu, Areeb Gani, Jacqueline Lu, Jordan Thomas, Mark Steyvers, Arman Cohan

Metacognition is a foundational component of intelligence critical to effective learning, problem solving, decision-making, communication, and more. In recent years, it has become increasingly recognized as a cornerstone of capable, transparent AI systems. Yet while LLMs have made significant progress across diverse real-world tasks, it is not yet clear when, how, or to what extent they can exhibit or be endowed with effective metacognitive abilities, nor how such abilities can be adapted to advance the fundamental capabilities, reliability, and intelligence of AI systems. This paper bridges this gap by presenting the first comprehensive overview of the current state of knowledge on metacognition for LLMs. We analyze and taxonomize the landscape of this emerging field and summarize recent technical advancements, including methods and benchmarks to measure and evaluate LLMs' metacognitive abilities, techniques to elicit, improve, and apply metacognition in LLMs, and findings and implications of ongoing research. We also discuss applications, open questions and challenges, and promising directions for future work. Our aim is to provide a detailed and up-to-date review of this topic and stimulate meaningful research and discussion. An organized list of papers can be found at https://github.com/yale-nlp/LLM-Metacognition.

摘要：元認知是智慧的基礎組成部分，對於有效的學習、問題解決、決策、溝通等至關重要。近年來，它越來越被認為是能夠且透明的人工智慧系統的基石。然而，儘管大型語言模型在多樣化的現實任務中取得了顯著進展，但目前尚不清楚它們何時、如何或在多大程度上能夠表現或被賦予有效的元認知能力，也不清楚這些能力如何能夠被調整以提升人工智慧系統的基本能力、可靠性和智慧。本文通過提供有關大型語言模型元認知的當前知識狀態的首次綜合概述來填補這一空白。我們分析並對這一新興領域的現狀進行分類，並總結了最近的技術進展，包括測量和評估大型語言模型元認知能力的方法和基準、在大型語言模型中引出、改善和應用元認知的技術，以及持續研究的發現和啟示。我們還討論了應用、未解決的問題和挑戰，以及未來工作的有希望的方向。我們的目標是提供這一主題的詳細和最新的回顧，並促進有意義的研究和討論。有組織的論文列表可以在 https://github.com/yale-nlp/LLM-Metacognition 找到。

##### **Invariant Learning Dynamics of Transformers in Inductive Reasoning Tasks**
2607.11875v1 by Tiberiu Musat, Tiago Pimentel, Nicholas Zucchet, Thomas Hofmann

We present a theoretical framework to explain the emergence of inductive reasoning abilities in Transformer language models. While previous works on Transformer learning dynamics have so far been mostly tied to specific tasks, we study a generalized class of inductive tasks that unifies several synthetic tasks known in the literature, including in-context n-grams and multi-hop reasoning. In this class, we theoretically prove that the training dynamics of attention models can be confined to a highly interpretable, low-dimensional invariant manifold. On this manifold, the learning dynamics are captured by a handful of interpretable coordinates rather than millions of parameters, making both theoretical and empirical analysis more tractable. Using this framework, we characterize how data statistics govern the competition between in-context and in-weights learning, we study how random initializations determine the `winning' circuit when multiple solutions are possible, and we demonstrate that the coordinate frame associated with the manifold can be used to automatically detect which circuits have been learned in trained models. By casting circuit formation as a low-dimensional dynamical phenomenon, we take a step toward a predictive theory of how Transformers learn.

摘要：研究人員將他們收集的論文組織成參考管理器中的個人資料夾層級，並將每篇新論文路由到其所屬的資料夾。這項任務與標準的層級文本分類不同。用戶的資料夾層級不是固定的、共享的分類法，而是一個私人的、演變中的民間分類法，其資料夾的意義可能是主題性的、簡寫的、基於會議的或以過程為導向的，並且通常由已存儲在其中的論文定義。我們將這一設置正式化為個性化層級論文路由（PHPR）：將進來的論文分配到用戶特定層級中的資料夾，而無需對每個用戶進行訓練。我們提出了PaperRouter-Agent，一種無需訓練的LLM代理，該代理在路由決策中基於資料夾成員而非僅僅是資料夾名稱。該代理首先縮小候選層級，檢索資料夾特定的證據，通過檢查成員論文來驗證適合度，並結合來自過去用戶拒絕的相似性閾值反饋。對真實個人圖書館的形成性研究顯示，PaperRouter-Agent將整體Recall@1從0.39提高到0.61，Recall@3從0.57提高到0.83，對於由元數據（如會議或年份）定義的組織資料夾的增益最大，而單次方法則崩潰（Recall@1從0.09提高到0.50）。在公共LaMP-2基準上，該方法將準確率從44.5%提高到51.5%（+9.0宏F1），同時保持低成本以便於實際使用。

##### **Technical Report on the CVPR 2026@AdvML Workshop Challenge**
2607.11560v1 by Tianyuan Zhang, Zonglei Jing, Jiangfan Liu, Ligong Zhang, Ke Ma, Chengzhi Sun, Xiaohai Xu, Zhirui Zhang, Qianqian Xu, Qingming Huang, Hanyu Fang, Junhua Liu, Zheng Wang, Xiaoliang Liu, Yuanbo Li, Shuai Gui, Bin Wang, Menghe Zheng, Jing Nie, Hanyang Meng, Zeyang Zhang, Xiang Zhang, Yongxuan Zhu, Rui Ding, Hainan Li, Yongkang Zhang, Zhilei Zhu, Xianglong Kong, Jin Hu, Zonghao Ying, Yisong Xiao, Lei Chen, Haotong Qin, Jiakai Wang, Aishan Liu, Ruikai Li, Julia Karbing, Yinpeng Dong, Zhenfei Yin, Shao Jing, Xia Hu, Jingyi Xu, Juntao Dai, Xinyun Chen, Vishal M. Patel, Xianglong Liu, Dawn Song, Alan Yuille, Philip H. S. Torr, Dacheng Tao

Vision-language agents (VLAs) are increasingly used to interpret complex driving scenes and support safety-critical reasoning. This report presents the CVPR 2026@AdvML Workshop Challenge on adversarial multimodal attacks against autonomous-driving VLAs. Built on DriveLM-style multi-view visual question answering, the challenge represents each scene with six synchronized camera images and a structured collection of driving-related question-answer pairs. Participants generate adversarial images and suffix-only textual perturbations that induce model responses to deviate from reference answers while preserving image fidelity and limiting textual cost. The competition comprises two phases, with Phase II adding a hidden black-box model to assess transferability. We describe the task design, submission rules, evaluation protocol, and leaderboard results, and then examine five leading submissions for which technical reports were available. Across these reports, several recurring patterns emerge: image-side attacks are favored by the suffix penalty; scene-level, multi-view optimization is more effective than treating views in isolation; QA types and graph structure provide useful priors for allocating attack budget; feature-space objectives can improve black-box transfer; and typographic content embedded in camera images exposes a persistent vulnerability in driving VLAs. These findings provide a practical reference for future robustness evaluation and defense design in multimodal autonomous-driving systems.

摘要：視覺-語言代理 (VLA) 正在越來越多地用於解釋複雜的駕駛場景並支持安全關鍵的推理。這份報告介紹了 CVPR 2026@AdvML 研討會挑戰，針對自主駕駛 VLA 的對抗性多模態攻擊。該挑戰基於 DriveLM 風格的多視角視覺問答，將每個場景表示為六個同步的攝影機圖像和一組結構化的駕駛相關問答對。參與者生成對抗性圖像和僅後綴的文本擾動，這些擾動使模型的回應偏離參考答案，同時保持圖像的真實性並限制文本成本。比賽分為兩個階段，第二階段增加了一個隱藏的黑箱模型來評估可轉移性。我們描述了任務設計、提交規則、評估協議和排行榜結果，然後檢視五個可用技術報告的領先提交。在這些報告中，出現了幾個重複的模式：圖像側的攻擊受到後綴懲罰的青睞；場景層級的多視角優化比單獨處理視角更有效；問答類型和圖形結構為分配攻擊預算提供了有用的先驗；特徵空間目標可以改善黑箱轉移；而嵌入在攝影機圖像中的排版內容揭示了駕駛 VLA 的持續脆弱性。這些發現為未來多模態自主駕駛系統的穩健性評估和防禦設計提供了實用的參考。

##### **Learning Residual Kinematic Corrections for Continuous Neural Decoding via Reinforcement Learning**

Deep learning architectures such as convolutional neural network--long short-term memory (CNN--LSTM) models can capture spatial and temporal dynamics for continuous kinematic decoding; however, systematic residual errors persist in predicted trajectories. We propose a two-stage decoding framework that applies reinforcement learning (RL) to perform residual kinematic correction on the outputs of a CNN--LSTM decoder (CNN--LSTM--RL). The RL agent is trained offline without direct EEG input and instead operates on predicted kinematic trajectories to optimize movement accuracy relative to target trajectories. Correspondingly, RMSE was reduced from $0.0890$ to $0.0532$ (2D, $p < 0.0001$) and from $0.0714$ to $0.0441$ (VR, $p < 0.0001$), representing relative reductions of $40.2\%$ and $38.2\%$. These findings demonstrate that this scalable framework enhances 3D BCI MI decoding by correcting kinematic errors via offline residual RL without extra neural data, advancing neurorehabilitation, prosthetics, and virtual interaction.

摘要：解碼連續三維 (3D) 動作意象 (MI) 使用非侵入性腦電圖 (EEG) 基於腦-電腦介面 (BCI) 仍然具有挑戰性，因為信號變異性和殘餘解碼錯誤。深度學習架構如卷積神經網絡-長短期記憶 (CNN-LSTM) 模型可以捕捉連續運動學解碼的空間和時間動態；然而，預測軌跡中仍然存在系統性的殘餘錯誤。我們提出了一個兩階段解碼框架，應用強化學習 (RL) 對 CNN-LSTM 解碼器 (CNN-LSTM-RL) 的輸出進行殘餘運動學修正。RL 代理在沒有直接 EEG 輸入的情況下進行離線訓練，而是基於預測的運動學軌跡來優化相對於目標軌跡的運動準確性。解碼性能通過皮爾森相關係數 ($r$) 和均方根誤差 (RMSE) 在 $x, y$ 和 $z$ 軸上進行量化。與單獨應用 CNN-LSTM 相比，CNN-LSTM-RL 在 2D 中將平均相關性從 $0.5076$ 提高到 $0.7181$ ($p = 0.0005$)，在 VR 中從 $0.6420$ 提高到 $0.7780$ ($p = 0.0059$)，相對增益分別為 $41.5\%$ 和 $21.2\%$。相應地，RMSE 從 $0.0890$ 降至 $0.0532$ (2D, $p < 0.0001$) 和從 $0.0714$ 降至 $0.0441$ (VR, $p < 0.0001$)，分別代表 $40.2\%$ 和 $38.2\%$ 的相對減少。這些發現表明，這個可擴展的框架通過離線殘餘 RL 修正運動學錯誤，增強了 3D BCI MI 解碼，無需額外的神經數據，推進了神經康復、義肢和虛擬互動。

##### **AutoMatBench: An Automatic Optimization Toolkit for the Acceleration of Material Properties Prediction Benchmarking**
2607.11526v1 by Hongxiao Li, Wanling Gao

Material property prediction (MPP) infers key properties from chemical composition and structure, accelerating the discovery and optimization of novel materials. In the realm of MPP, MatBench is a widely accepted benchmarking tool that defines over ten significant problems and provides the paradigm of performance evaluation for AI prediction models. Even though MatBench works well in benchmarking the performances of prediction models on in-distribution (ID) tasks and datasets, it lacks the ability to reflect their performances on out-of-distribution (OOD) material data, resulting failure in new material discovery. By combining the pipelines of MatBench and the existing researches on OOD performance evaluation, this study enables a huge space of benchmarking configurations, comprehensively reflecting the performances, abilities, and disadvantages of various AI prediction models. This work reports that the discrepancy of performances at different configuration values is huge and can be illustrated with prior knowledge and novel insights, therefore consideration of causal effect of configurations on performance results is necessary. In case of the impossibility of enumerative benchmarking at every configuration, this work further proposes AutoMatBench, an automatic toolkit with Bayesian optimization. Experiments with AutoMatBench reports that, within twelve steps of optimization, the similar results with MatBench and former OOD research can be accessed while more than half of the cost are saved. Besides, this tool also yields more essential findings on MPP benchmarking, positively contributing to the cost and efficiency of new material discovery.

摘要：材料性質預測 (MPP) 從化學成分和結構推斷關鍵性質，加速新材料的發現和優化。<lb/>在MPP領域，MatBench是一個被廣泛接受的基準工具，定義了十多個重要問題，並提供了AI預測模型性能評估的範式。<lb/>儘管MatBench在基準測試預測模型在內部分佈 (ID) 任務和數據集的性能方面表現良好，但它缺乏反映其在外部分佈 (OOD) 材料數據上性能的能力，導致在新材料發現上的失敗。<lb/>通過結合MatBench的流程和現有的OOD性能評估研究，本研究實現了大量的基準配置空間，全面反映各種AI預測模型的性能、能力和缺點。<lb/>本研究報告不同配置值下性能的差異巨大，並且可以用先前的知識和新穎的見解來說明，因此有必要考慮配置對性能結果的因果影響。<lb/>在無法對每個配置進行枚舉基準測試的情況下，本研究進一步提出了AutoMatBench，一個具有貝葉斯優化的自動工具包。<lb/>使用AutoMatBench的實驗報告，在十二步優化內，可以獲得與MatBench和之前的OOD研究相似的結果，同時節省了超過一半的成本。<lb/>此外，這個工具還產生了對MPP基準測試更重要的發現，對新材料發現的成本和效率做出了積極貢獻。

##### **Vinci2: Providing Proactive Assistance in Continuous Egocentric Videos**
2607.11523v1 by Gong Sitong, Tianyu Yan, Caixin Kang, Bo Zheng, Xiang Ruan, Huchuan Lu, Kaipeng Zhang, Yoichi Sato, Yifei Huang

When should an intelligent assistant speak up without being asked? Continuous egocentric video offers rich, evolving context that enables a new form of assistance: one that is proactive rather than merely reactive. Yet existing approaches either wait passively for user queries or treat every detected event as requiring a response, without considering the user's history, current activity, or whether assistance would actually be welcome. We reframe proactive assistance as a context-dependent decision problem: the agent must not only perceive what is happening, but reason over accumulated temporal context to determine when and whether to intervene. To this end, we present Vinci2, a proactive egocentric assistance system that advances the on-device assistant Vinci from reactive response toward proactivity. On the evaluation side, we present EgoServe, the first large-scale benchmark for proactive assistance in continuous egocentric video. EgoServe comprises over 3,000 service instances organized along 4 temporal memory horizons, ranging from immediate safety alerts to long-term habit coaching, across 10 service categories. On the modeling side, we propose EgoMemo, a training-free, memory-augmented agent that maintains three complementary memory representations: multi-scale temporal summaries, a semantic knowledge graph, and visual embedding archives. At each timestep, EgoMemo performs retrieval-augmented reasoning to determine whether assistance is warranted and, if so, produces contextually grounded responses. Experiments demonstrate that EgoMemo establishes strong baselines on EgoServe while remaining competitive on existing egocentric benchmarks. Our benchmark and code are publicly available at \href{https://sitonggong.github.io/EgoServe-page/}{Vinci2}.

摘要：當智能助手應該在未被要求的情況下主動發言？持續的自我中心視頻提供了豐富且不斷演變的背景，使一種新的協助形式成為可能：這種形式是主動的，而不僅僅是被動反應。然而，現有的方法要麼被動等待用戶查詢，要麼將每個檢測到的事件視為需要回應的情況，而不考慮用戶的歷史、當前活動或是否實際上會歡迎協助。我們將主動協助重新框架為一個依賴於上下文的決策問題：代理不僅必須感知正在發生的事情，還必須在累積的時間上下文中進行推理，以確定何時以及是否介入。為此，我們提出了Vinci2，一個主動的自我中心協助系統，將反應式助手Vinci推進到主動性。 在評估方面，我們提出了EgoServe，這是第一個針對持續自我中心視頻的主動協助的大型基準。EgoServe包含超過3,000個服務實例，按照4個時間記憶範疇組織，範圍從即時安全警報到長期習慣指導，涵蓋10個服務類別。在建模方面，我們提出了EgoMemo，一個無需訓練的增強記憶代理，維持三種互補的記憶表示：多尺度時間摘要、語義知識圖和視覺嵌入檔案。在每個時間步，EgoMemo執行檢索增強推理，以確定是否需要協助，如果需要，則產生上下文相關的回應。實驗表明，EgoMemo在EgoServe上建立了強大的基準，同時在現有的自我中心基準上保持競爭力。我們的基準和代碼在\href{https://sitonggong.github.io/EgoServe-page/}{Vinci2}上公開可用。

However, documenting Dzongkha writing is a challenging and time-consuming process, largely due to the complexity of the script, the need for multiple keystrokes per syllable, and the limited availability of efficient typing tools. An immediate system that can predict and display a list of probable words for Dzongkha is the solution for this problem. The project is mainly aimed to make Dzongkha typing as convenient as possible by reducing the number of keystrokes. The training process included fine-tuning of the model's hyperparameters. 摘要：Dzongkha，作為不丹的國語，是該國一種常見且廣泛使用的語言。<lb/>官方文件、經文和其他文學作品都是用Dzongkha撰寫，以保持其文化價值。<lb/>然而，記錄Dzongkha的書寫是一個具有挑戰性且耗時的過程，主要是由於字母的複雜性、每個音節需要多次按鍵以及高效打字工具的有限可用性。<lb/>一個能夠預測並顯示Dzongkha可能單詞列表的即時系統是解決這個問題的方案。<lb/>該項目的主要目的是通過減少按鍵數量，使Dzongkha的打字變得盡可能方便。<lb/>我們的數據集來自DCDD，共有100000個句子、1331282個單詞和28344個獨特單詞。<lb/>數據預處理的過程包括去除所有字母數字字符、標記化、生成N-gram序列和填充。<lb/>選擇的三個訓練模型是LSTM、Bi-LSTM和GRU。<lb/>訓練過程包括對模型超參數的微調。<lb/>GRU因為輕量且能處理更大的數據集，表現最佳，準確率達到74.03%，並且解決了過擬合問題。

##### **CDFM: Towards a General-Purpose Causal Discovery Foundation Model**
2607.11508v1 by Jie Qiao, Ruichu Cai, Zijian Li, Weilin Chen, Pengfei Hua, Boyan Xu, Zhengming Chen, Zhifeng Hao, Peng Cui

Causal discovery, the process of recovering underlying causal structures from observational data, is a fundamental pursuit across scientific disciplines. Over the past decades, numerous algorithms have been developed to tackle this challenge through workflows tailored to the specific causal mechanisms underlying each type of dataset, demonstrating effectiveness across a wide range of applications. However, as the volume and heterogeneity of real-world data continue to grow, this dataset-specific approach inevitably leads to a fragmented, test-driven paradigm that struggles to scale to the demands of modern scientific discovery. To address this, we formulate the Causal Discovery Foundation Model (CDFM) as a unified, general-purpose framework for zero-shot structural inference. To ensure reliable generalization across unknown domains, we first investigate the theoretical boundaries of causal identifiability, revealing the indispensable role of causal prior mechanisms in this process. Building on these insights, we formulate a principled variational framework that treats unknown causal mechanisms as latent variables and mathematically decomposes the intractable marginal likelihood into distinct, tractable learning modules. The variational decomposition provides a conceptual design principle for the architecture design of CDFM, while comprehensive causal knowledge guides the large-scale synthesis of our pretraining data. By pretraining on a massive, highly diverse space of synthetic structural causal models, CDFM successfully internalizes complex statistical asymmetries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CDFM consistently outperforms traditional algorithms, driving a paradigm shift toward a general-purpose causal discovery foundation model.

摘要：因果發現，即從觀察數據中恢復潛在因果結構的過程，是各科學領域中的一項基本追求。<lb/>在過去幾十年中，已經開發了許多算法來應對這一挑戰，通過針對每種類型數據集的特定因果機制量身定制的工作流程，展示了在廣泛應用中的有效性。<lb/>然而，隨著現實世界數據的體量和異質性不斷增長，這種特定數據集的方法不可避免地導致了一種支離破碎的、以測試為驅動的範式，難以滿足現代科學發現的需求。<lb/>為了解決這個問題，我們將因果發現基礎模型（CDFM）制定為一個統一的、通用的零樣本結構推斷框架。<lb/>為了確保在未知領域的可靠泛化，我們首先研究因果可識別性的理論邊界，揭示因果先驗機制在此過程中的不可或缺的角色。<lb/>基於這些見解，我們制定了一個原則性的變分框架，將未知因果機制視為潛在變量，並將難以處理的邊際似然數學分解為不同的、可處理的學習模塊。<lb/>變分分解為CDFM的架構設計提供了一個概念設計原則，而全面的因果知識則指導我們的預訓練數據的大規模合成。<lb/>通過在一個龐大且高度多樣的合成結構因果模型空間中進行預訓練，CDFM成功地內化了複雜的統計不對稱性。<lb/>大量實驗表明，CDFM始終優於傳統算法，推動了向通用因果發現基礎模型的範式轉變。

##### **SCOPE-RL: Optimizing Reasoning Paths Before and After Success**
2607.11506v1 by Xiaojian Liu, Han Xu, Jianqiang Xia, Zhixuan Li, Ke Xu, Yiwei Dai, Xinran Chen, Changwo Wu, Yuchen Li

Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) optimizes LLMs using sparse verifiable final-answer rewards. This sparse anchor reliably verifies whether a trajectory succeeds but provides no direct feedback on the reasoning path that produced it. Before success, prerequisite progress on hard problems receives no reward signal; after success, outcome rewards cannot distinguish well-organized correct trajectories from redundant or locally flawed ones. 摘要：大多數手語翻譯（SLT）方法專注於孤立的母語手語-口語對（例如，美國手語 - 英語）。將特定語言的 SLT 模型擴展到多語言翻譯將通過促進不同手語和口語社群之間的交流來改善可及性。然而，現有的多語言 SLT 方法仍然難以學習一個統一的模型，以最小化跨語言衝突，同時捕捉共享的跨語言語義並保留不同手語之間的語言特定變異。因此，我們提出了 Q-BridgeNet，一個統一的多語言 SLT 框架，旨在共同減輕手語和口語兩側的跨語言衝突。在手語方面，Q-BridgeNet 通過自適應分段和殘差向量量化學習離散的 Q 單元：一個共享的基本代碼本提供語言無關的語義原語，而語言特定的殘差代碼本則細化異質的手語語義。在口語方面，一個多語言 LLM 被微調以在 Q 單元空間中運作，利用跨語言先驗來實現統一的 SLT 模型。在 PHOENIX14T、How2Sign 和 CSL-Daily 上的實驗顯示，Q-BridgeNet 有效減輕了跨語言衝突，在母語手語-口語對上達到了最先進的性能，同時也展現了對非母語對的強大泛化能力。我們的源代碼已公開可用於：https://github.com/FengLiQ/Q-BridgeNet

##### **When the Target Domain Changes: AI-Mediated Construct Drift in High-Stakes English Language AssessmenW**
2607.11213v1 by Yi Gui

High-stakes English proficiency tests treat standardized, unaided performance as evidence for score interpretations about academic English proficiency. This interpretation remains meaningful, but as target language use domains increasingly involve generative AI, the extrapolation from unaided test performance to academic communicative readiness becomes less self-evident. This conceptual validity argument reframes AI as a score-interpretation problem in high-stakes language testing, not only an operational issue of scoring, feedback, security, or misconduct. Synthesizing current literature in three uneven layers, the paper shows that most work treats AI as assessment infrastructure, while far less theorizes its implications for construct validity and extrapolation warrants. It defines AI-mediated construct drift as the misalignment that arises when communicative abilities required in the target domain change through AI mediation while test constructs remain anchored to an unaided-performance model. It proposes bounded AI mediation as a validity-oriented design principle: a standardized condition in which all test takers access the same institutionally controlled AI assistant, with predefined assistance boundaries, logged interactions, and tasks that distinguish comprehension support from answer generation. The paper argues that score interpretations should be narrowed and supplemented when used to support claims about AI-mediated academic communication.

摘要：高風險的英語能力測試將標準化、未經協助的表現視為學術英語能力評分解釋的證據。這種解釋仍然具有意義，但隨著目標語言使用領域越來越多地涉及生成式人工智慧，從未經協助的測試表現推斷學術交流準備的過程變得不再那麼顯而易見。這一概念有效性論證將人工智慧重新框架為高風險語言測試中的評分解釋問題，而不僅僅是評分、反饋、安全或不當行為的操作性問題。通過三個不均勻的層次綜合當前文獻，本文顯示大多數研究將人工智慧視為評估基礎設施，而對其對構念有效性和推斷授權的影響則理論探討得較少。它將人工智慧介導的構念漂移定義為當目標領域所需的交流能力通過人工智慧介導而改變，而測試構念仍然固定於未經協助的表現模型時所產生的不一致。它提出以有限的人工智慧介導作為一種以有效性為導向的設計原則：一種標準化條件，在此條件下，所有考生都可以訪問相同的機構控制的人工智慧助手，並具有預定的協助邊界、記錄的互動和區分理解支持與答案生成的任務。本文主張，當用於支持關於人工智慧介導的學術交流的主張時，應縮小並補充評分解釋。

However, a series of issues still constrain the application of this task. When LLMs exhibit uneven performance across planning tasks, these gaps are often attributed to task difficulty. We argue that this explanation is incomplete, as task-level variation may reflect distinct latent planning competencies rather than differences along a single ability spectrum. ##### **RepTran: Search-Based Repair of Transformer Models**
2607.11193v1 by Yuta Ishimoto, Paolo Arcaini, Fuyuki Ishikawa, Masanari Kondo, Naoyasu Ubayashi, Yasutaka Kamei

To ensure the overall quality of AI-enabled software, not only traditional software components but also AI components need to be tested and repaired. Among AI components, Transformer models are increasingly integrated into software systems, which makes their misbehaviors critical. Although prior work in the software engineering community has proposed deep neural network (DNN) repair methods, most overlook Transformer-specific structures.
